\displaystyle[x^{\mu}\partial_{\mu},\partial_{\alpha}]=-\delta_{{\alpha}\mu}\partial_{\mu}=-\partial_{\alpha}\,.

(10.38)

Consequently, for all
Z\in{\cal Z}
, we have in wave coordinates that

\displaystyle{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\alpha}}K_{\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\alpha}}K_{\beta}

(10.39)

\displaystyle{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}K_{{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\beta}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}K_{{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\beta}}.

(10.40)

Hence, for all
Z\in{\cal Z}
, in wave coordinates, we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}K_{\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Z\partial_{\alpha}K_{\beta}-\partial_{{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\alpha}}K_{\beta}-\partial_{\alpha}K_{{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\beta}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Z\partial_{\alpha}K_{\beta}-\partial_{[Z,e_{\alpha}]}K_{\beta}-\partial_{\alpha}K_{{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\beta}}.

On the other hand, let’s compute
{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}K_{\beta})={\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}K)_{\beta}
, where the differentiation treats
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}K
as a one-tensor. We have in wave coordinates,

\displaystyle{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}K)_{\beta}=\partial_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}K_{\beta})\,.

However,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}K_{\beta}=\partial_{Z}K_{\beta}-K_{{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\beta}}\,.

Thus,

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}K_{\beta})=\partial_{\alpha}(ZK_{\beta})-\partial_{\alpha}(K_{{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\beta}}).

Yet,

\displaystyle[Z,e_{\alpha}]K_{\beta}=Z\partial_{\alpha}K_{\beta}-\partial_{\alpha}ZK_{\beta}

and hence, for all
Z\in\cal Z
, we have

\displaystyle{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}K)_{\beta}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}K_{\beta})=Z\partial_{\alpha}K_{\beta}-[Z,e_{\alpha}]K_{\beta}-\partial_{\alpha}K_{{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\beta}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}K_{\beta}.

By induction on
|I|
, we get that for all products of Lie derivatives
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}
, the following equality holds,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}K_{\beta}={\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}K_{\beta})\;.

(10.41)

Lemma 10.6
.

The Lie derivative in the direction of the Minkowski vector fields of the Minkowski metric, is either null or proportional to the Minkowski metric, that is for any
Z\in{\cal Z}
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}m_{\mu\nu}=c_{Z}\cdot m_{\mu\nu}

(10.42)

and

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}m^{\mu\nu}=-c_{Z}\cdot m^{\mu\nu}

(10.43)

where
c_{Z}=0
for all
Z\neq S
and
c_{S}=2
.

Thus,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}m_{\mu\nu}=c(I)\cdot m_{\mu\nu}\;

(10.44)

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}m^{\mu\nu}=\hat{c}(I)\cdot m^{\mu\nu}\;,

(10.45)

where
c(I)
and
\hat{c}(I)
are constants that depend on
Z^{I}
.

Proof.

We compute in wave coordinates
\mu,\nu\in\{x^{0},x^{1},\ldots,x^{n}\}

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}m_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Zm_{\mu\nu}-m({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\mu},e_{\nu})-m(e_{\mu},{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}e_{\nu})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-m([Z,e_{\mu}],e_{\nu})-m(e_{\mu},[Z,e_{\nu}])\;.

Case of rotations:

We already showed in (
10.36
), that

\displaystyle[Z_{ij},e_{\mu}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{\mu j}\partial_{i}-\delta_{\mu i}\partial_{j}\;.

Hence,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z_{ij}}m_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-m(\delta_{\mu j}\partial_{i}-\delta_{\mu i}\partial_{j},e_{\nu})-m(e_{\mu},\delta_{\nu j}\partial_{i}-\delta_{\nu i}\partial_{j})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\delta_{\mu j}m_{i\nu}+\delta_{\mu i}m_{j\nu}-\delta_{\nu j}m_{i\mu}+\delta_{\nu i}m_{j\mu}\;.

Consequently, if
i\neq j
and if
\mu=j\neq i
and if
\nu=j\neq i
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z_{ij}}m_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-m_{i\nu}-m_{i\mu}=0+0

\displaystyle\text{(since $\mu\neq i$ and $\nu\neq i$}).

Now, if
i\neq j
and if
\mu=j\neq i
and if
\nu=i\neq j
, then

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z_{ij}}m_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-m_{i\nu}+m_{j\mu}=-m_{ii}+m_{jj}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-1+1=0\;.

Now, if
i\neq j
and if
\mu=t
, then clearly

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z_{ij}}m_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;.

Of course, in the case where
i=j
, then
Z_{ij}=0
and therefore
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z_{ij}}m_{\mu\nu}=0
.

Case of Lorentz boosts:

We showed in (
10.37
), that

\displaystyle[Z_{0j},e_{\mu}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{\mu j}\partial_{t}+\delta_{\mu 0}\partial_{j}\;.

Thus,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z_{0j}}m_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-m(\delta_{\mu j}\partial_{t}+\delta_{\mu 0}\partial_{j},e_{\nu})-m(e_{\mu},\delta_{\nu j}\partial_{t}+\delta_{\nu 0}\partial_{j})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\delta_{\mu j}m_{t\nu}-\delta_{\mu 0}m_{j\nu}-\delta_{\nu j}m_{\mu t}-\delta_{\nu 0}m_{\mu j}\;.

Hence, if
\mu=t\neq j
, then

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z_{0j}}m_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-m_{j\nu}-\delta_{\nu j}m_{tt}=-m_{j\nu}+\delta_{\nu j}\;,

and therefore if
\nu=t
then
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z_{0j}}m_{\mu\nu}=0
and if
\nu=i
spatial index, then
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z_{0j}}m_{\mu\nu}=-\delta_{ji}+\delta_{ij}=0
.

Now considering the case where
\mu=i
, then

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z_{0j}}m_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\delta_{ij}m_{t\nu}-\delta_{\nu 0}m_{ij}=-\delta_{ij}\delta_{\nu 0}-\delta_{\nu 0}\delta_{ij}=0\;.

Case of the scaling vector field:

We have shown in (
10.38
), that

\displaystyle[S,e_{\mu}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{\mu}\;.

Hence,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{S}m_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{\mu\nu}+m_{\mu\nu}=2m_{\mu\nu}\;.

Since the end result are identities which are tensorial, they are therefore true not only in wave coordinates (yet, we have carried out the computation in wave coordinates).

Case of the contravariant tensor
m^{\mu\nu}
:

We have

\displaystyle m_{\mu{\beta}}\cdot m^{{\beta}\nu}=\delta_{\mu}^{\;\;\;\nu}

Using the fact that the Lie derivative commutes with contraction, that is

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}(m_{\mu{\beta}}\cdot m^{{\beta}\nu})=0\;,

yields to

\displaystyle({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}m_{\mu{\beta}})\cdot m^{{\beta}\nu}+m_{\mu{\beta}}\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}m^{{\beta}\nu}=0\;,

and thus,

\displaystyle c_{Z}m_{\mu{\beta}}\cdot m^{{\beta}\nu}+m_{\mu{\beta}}\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}m^{{\beta}\nu}=0\;,

and therefore,

\displaystyle m_{\mu{\beta}}\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}m^{{\beta}\nu}=-c_{Z}\delta_{\mu}^{\;\;\;\nu}\;.

Inverting, this leads to

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}m^{{\beta}\nu}=-c_{Z}m^{{\beta}\nu}\;.

Case for higher order Lie derivatives:

The equalities (
10.44
) and (
10.45
) follow by trivial induction, from which we get the desired result.

Lemma 10.7
.

We have for any family of covariant tensors
K^{(1)},\ldots,K^{(m)}
of arbitrary order,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}O(K^{(1)}\cdot\ldots\cdot K^{(m)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|J_{1}|+\ldots+|J_{m}|\leq|I|}O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J_{1}}}K^{(1)}\cdot\ldots\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J_{m}}}K^{(m)})\;.

Proof.

We have that
O(K^{(1)}\cdot\ldots\cdot K^{(m)})
is a product of metrics
m
and the inverse metric
\textbf{m}^{-1}
and the tensors
K^{(1)},\;\ldots,K^{(m)}
, times any polynomial of these. Using chain rule for the Lie derivative and the fact the Lie derivative in the direction of Minkowski vector fields of the metric
m
and of the contravariant metric
\textbf{m}^{-1}
is proportional to these, we then get that the Lie derivatives of the tensors
K^{(1)},\;\ldots,K^{(m)}
and
m
and of
\textbf{m}^{-1}
is contained in the product of all the Lie derivatives of these. The Lie derivatives of any polynomial of
K^{(1)},\;\ldots,K^{(m)}
and
m
and of
\textbf{m}^{-1}
is also a product of the Lie derivatives of these. Given that the Lie derivative of
m
is proportional to
m
and the Lie derivative of
\textbf{m}^{-1}
is proportional to
\textbf{m}^{-1}
, the multiplication of all these Lie derivatives are contained in the definition of
\sum_{|J_{1}|+\ldots+|J_{m}|\leq|I|}O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J_{1}}}K^{(1)}\cdot\ldots\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J_{m}}}K^{(m)})
.

Lemma 10.8
.

In the Lorenz gauge and in wave coordinates, we have for any
V\in\cal U

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{V})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))+O({\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))

\displaystyle+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A_{L}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A_{\cal{T}}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A_{\cal{T}}))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)\;\big)\,.

Proof.

In the Lorenz gauge and in wave coordinates, we have shown that for any
V\in\cal U

\displaystyle g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{V}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(A\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}A)

\displaystyle+O(A_{L}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}A)+O(A_{\cal{T}}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}A_{\cal{T}})+O(A^{3})

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(h\cdot A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot A^{3})\,.

Differentiating, we get for any
Z\in\cal Z
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{V})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(A\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}A)

\displaystyle+O(A_{L}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}A)+O(A_{\cal{T}}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}A_{\cal{T}})+O(A^{3})

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(h\cdot A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot A^{3})

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A_{L}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}A)

\displaystyle+O(A_{L}\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A_{\cal{T}}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}A_{\cal{T}})+O(A_{\cal{T}}\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}A_{\cal{T}})

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot A^{2})+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h\cdot A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h\cdot A^{3})+O(h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot A^{2})\,.

By induction, we obtain that for all
Z^{I}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{V})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A_{L}\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A_{\cal{T}}\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{V}A_{\cal{T}})+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)\;\big)\,.

Using the fact that
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}
commutes with
{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}
, we obtain the result.

Lemma 10.9
.

In the Lorenz and harmonic gauges, we have the following estimate for the tangential components of the Einstein-Yang-Mills potential,

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{{\cal T}})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|)+ O(|{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|)\;\big)\,.

Proof.

Based on what we have shown, we have for all
Z^{I}
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{{\cal T}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))+O({\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))

\displaystyle+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(A\cdot{\mbox{$\nabla\mkern-13.0mu/$\,}^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A\;\big)\,.

11.
Studying the structure of the source terms of the coupled non-linear wave equations

In this section, we study the general structure of the source terms of the coupled non-linear wave equations on the Yang-Mills potential and the metric in the Lorenz gauge and in wave coordinates.

Lemma 11.1
.

In the Lorenz gauge, the Yang-Mills potential satisfies the following tensorial equations, where we lower and higher indices with respect to the metric
m
,

\displaystyle g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{\sigma}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}h^{{\alpha}\mu})\cdot({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{\mu})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\big({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{\mu}h^{\nu}_{\,\,\,\sigma}+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}h^{\nu\mu}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{\nu}h^{\mu}_{\,\,\,\sigma}\big)\cdot\big({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{\nu}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}A_{\mu}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\big({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{\mu}h^{\nu}_{\,\,\,\sigma}+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}h^{\nu\mu}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{\nu}h^{\mu}_{\,\,\,\sigma}\big)\cdot[A_{\mu},A_{\nu}]

\displaystyle-\big([A_{\mu},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{\mu}A_{\sigma}]+[A^{\mu},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{\sigma}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}A_{\mu}]+[A^{\mu},[A_{\mu},A_{\sigma}]]\big)

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(h\cdot A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot A^{3})\,.

The perturbations
h
of the metric
m
, solutions to the Einstein-Yang-Mills equations in theLorenz gauge, satisfy the following tensorial wave equation, where we lower and higher indices with respect to the metric
m
,

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\beta}h_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h,{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}h)+Q_{\mu\nu}({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h,{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h)+G_{\mu\nu}(h)({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h,{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h)

\displaystyle-4<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{\mu},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A_{\nu}>

\displaystyle+m_{\mu\nu}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{{\alpha}},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A^{{\alpha}}>

\displaystyle-4\cdot\big(<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{\mu},[A_{\nu},A^{{\beta}}]>+<[A_{\mu},A_{{\beta}}],{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A_{\nu}>\big)

\displaystyle+m_{\mu\nu}\cdot\big(<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{{\alpha}},[A^{{\alpha}},A^{{\beta}}]>+<[A_{{\alpha}},A_{{\beta}}],{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\alpha}}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A^{{\alpha}}>\big)

\displaystyle-4<[A_{\mu},A_{{\beta}}],[A_{\nu},A^{{\beta}}]>+m_{\mu\nu}\cdot<[A_{{\alpha}},A_{{\beta}}],[A^{{\alpha}},A^{{\beta}}]>

\displaystyle+O\big(h\cdot({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)^{2}\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{2}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{4}\big)\,,

where
P
,
Q
and
G
are defined in (
7.7
), (
7.3
) and (
7.9
).

Remark 11.1
.

As a reminder,
m
is defined to be the Minkowski metric in wave coordinates.

Proof.

We showed in Lemma
7.4
, that in the Lorenz gauge and in wave coordinates, in other words for indices running only over wave coordinates, i.e.
\lambda,\mu,\sigma,{\beta},\nu,{\alpha}\in\{t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n}\}
, the Yang-Mills potential satisfies

\displaystyle g^{\lambda\mu}\partial_{\lambda}\partial_{\mu}A_{\sigma}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{{\alpha}\gamma}m^{\mu\lambda}(\partial_{\sigma}h_{\gamma\lambda})\partial_{{\alpha}}A_{\mu}+\frac{1}{2}m^{{\alpha}\mu}m^{{\beta}\nu}\big(\partial_{\alpha}h_{{\beta}\sigma}+\partial_{\sigma}h_{{\beta}{\alpha}}-\partial_{\beta}h_{{\alpha}\sigma}\big)\cdot\big(\partial_{\mu}A_{\nu}-\partial_{\nu}A_{\mu}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}m^{{\alpha}\mu}m^{{\beta}\nu}\big(\partial_{\alpha}h_{{\beta}\sigma}+\partial_{\sigma}h_{{\beta}{\alpha}}-\partial_{\beta}h_{{\alpha}\sigma}\big)\cdot[A_{\mu},A_{\nu}]

\displaystyle-m^{{\alpha}\mu}\big([A_{\mu},\partial_{{\alpha}}A_{\sigma}]+[A_{\alpha},\partial_{\mu}A_{\sigma}-\partial_{\sigma}A_{\mu}]+[A_{\alpha},[A_{\mu},A_{\sigma}]]\big)

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot\partial h\cdot\partial A)+O(h\cdot\partial h\cdot A^{2})+O(h\cdot A\cdot\partial A)+O(h\cdot A^{3})\,.

Since the Christoffel symbols for the connection
{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}
are vanishing in wave coordinates, we could then write,

\displaystyle g^{\lambda\mu}\partial_{\lambda}\partial_{\mu}A_{\sigma}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{{\alpha}\gamma}m^{\mu\lambda}({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}h_{\gamma\lambda}){\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{\mu}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}m^{{\alpha}\mu}m^{{\beta}\nu}\big({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}h_{{\beta}\sigma}+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}h_{{\beta}{\alpha}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\beta}h_{{\alpha}\sigma}\big)\cdot\big({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{\nu}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}A_{\mu}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}m^{{\alpha}\mu}m^{{\beta}\nu}\big({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}h_{{\beta}\sigma}+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}h_{{\beta}{\alpha}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\beta}h_{{\alpha}\sigma}\big)\cdot[A_{\mu},A_{\nu}]

\displaystyle-m^{{\alpha}\mu}\big([A_{\mu},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{\sigma}]+[A_{\alpha},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{\sigma}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}A_{\mu}]+[A_{\alpha},[A_{\mu},A_{\sigma}]]\big)

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot\partial h\cdot\partial A)+O(h\cdot\partial h\cdot A^{2})+O(h\cdot A\cdot\partial A)+O(h\cdot A^{3})\,.

Also, we have

\displaystyle g^{\lambda\mu}\partial_{\lambda}\partial_{\mu}A_{\sigma}=g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{\sigma},

which is a tensor in
\sigma
. Thus, the right hand side and the left hand side of the following equation is a tensor in
\sigma
and corresponds to a full tensorial contraction on all other indices and hence the expression does not depend on the system of coordinates that we choose.

By lowering and highering indices with respect to the metric
m
, defined to be the Minkowski metric in wave coordinates, we get the result for wave equation satisfied for
A_{\sigma}
.

Similarly, we showed that in wave coordinates, the metric solution to the Einstein-Yang-Mills equations in the Lorenz gauge satisfies the following equation,

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\beta}h_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P(\partial_{\mu}h,\partial_{\nu}h)+Q_{\mu\nu}(\partial h,\partial h)+G_{\mu\nu}(h)(\partial h,\partial h)

\displaystyle-4m^{\sigma{\beta}}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{\mu},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}A_{\sigma}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}A_{\nu}>

\displaystyle+m_{\mu\nu}m^{\sigma{\beta}}m^{{\alpha}\lambda}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{{\alpha}},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}A_{\sigma}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}A_{\lambda}>

\displaystyle-4m^{\sigma{\beta}}\cdot\big(<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{\mu},[A_{\nu},A_{\sigma}]>+<[A_{\mu},A_{{\beta}}],{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}A_{\sigma}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}A_{\nu}>\big)

\displaystyle+m_{\mu\nu}m^{\sigma{\beta}}m^{{\alpha}\lambda}\cdot\big(<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{{\alpha}},[A_{\lambda},A_{\sigma}]>+<[A_{{\alpha}},A_{{\beta}}],{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}A_{\sigma}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}A_{\lambda}>\big)

\displaystyle-4m^{\sigma{\beta}}\cdot<[A_{\mu},A_{{\beta}}],[A_{\nu},A_{\sigma}]>+m_{\mu\nu}m^{\sigma{\beta}}m^{{\alpha}\lambda}\cdot<[A_{{\alpha}},A_{{\beta}}],[A_{\lambda},A_{\sigma}]>

\displaystyle+O\big(h\cdot(\partial A)^{2}\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{2}\cdot\partial A\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{4}\big)\,.

Again, by lowering and highering indices with respect to the metric
m
, we obtain the result for the wave equation satisfied for
h_{\mu\nu}
.

Lemma 11.2
.

In the Lorenz gauge, we have for any
V\in\{\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{\mu}}\,\,|\,\,\mu\in\{0,1,\ldots,n\}\}
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{V})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)\;\big)\,,

and therefore,

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|)+O(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|)\;\big)\,.

Proof.

In the Lorenz gauge, we have shown that we have,

\displaystyle g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{\sigma}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}h^{{\alpha}\mu})\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{\mu}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\big({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{\mu}h^{\nu}_{\,\,\,\sigma}+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}h^{\nu\mu}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{\nu}h^{\mu}_{\,\,\,\sigma}\big)\cdot\big({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{\nu}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}A_{\mu}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\big({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{\mu}h^{\nu}_{\,\,\,\sigma}+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}h^{\nu\mu}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{\nu}h^{\mu}_{\,\,\,\sigma}\big)\cdot[A_{\mu},A_{\nu}]

\displaystyle-\big([A_{\mu},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{\mu}A_{\sigma}]+[A^{\mu},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{\sigma}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\sigma}A_{\mu}]+[A^{\mu},[A_{\mu},A_{\sigma}]]\big)

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot\partial h\cdot\partial A)+O(h\cdot\partial h\cdot A^{2})+O(h\cdot A\cdot\partial A)+O(h\cdot A^{3})\,,

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(A^{3})

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(h\cdot A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot A^{3})\,.

Differentiating, and using Definition
9.4
, we get for any
Z\in\cal Z
, and for any wave coordinate vector
V
, and using Definition
9.4

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{V})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(A^{3})

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(h\cdot A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot A^{3})

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot A^{2})+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)

\displaystyle+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot A^{2})+O(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h\cdot A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O(h\cdot A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h\cdot A^{3})+O(h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}A\cdot A^{2})\,.

By induction, we obtain that for all
Z^{I}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{V})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)\;\big)\,.

Using the fact that
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}
commutes with
{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}
, we obtain the result.

Lemma 11.3
.

We have for any
U,V\in\{\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{\mu}}\,\,|\,\,\mu\in\{0,1,\ldots,n\}\}
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h_{UV})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}h))

\displaystyle+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A))

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{N}}A)\;\big)\,,

and therefore,

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}h)|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A)|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{N}}A|)\;\big)\,.

Proof.

We showed that

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\beta}h_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P(\partial_{\mu}h,\partial_{\nu}h)+Q_{\mu\nu}(\partial h,\partial h)+G_{\mu\nu}(h)(\partial h,\partial h)

\displaystyle-4<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{\mu},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A_{\nu}>

\displaystyle+m_{\mu\nu}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{{\alpha}},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A^{{\alpha}}>

\displaystyle-4\cdot\big(<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{\mu},[A_{\nu},A^{{\beta}}]>+<[A_{\mu},A_{{\beta}}],{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A_{\nu}>\big)

\displaystyle+m_{\mu\nu}\cdot\big(<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{{\alpha}},[A^{{\alpha}},A^{{\beta}}]>+<[A_{{\alpha}},A_{{\beta}}],{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\alpha}}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A^{{\alpha}}>\big)

\displaystyle-4<[A_{\mu},A_{{\beta}}],[A_{\nu},A^{{\beta}}]>+m_{\mu\nu}\cdot<[A_{{\alpha}},A_{{\beta}}],[A^{{\alpha}},A^{{\beta}}]>

\displaystyle+O\big(h\cdot(\partial A)^{2}\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{2}\cdot\partial A\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{4}\big)\,.

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h,{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}h)+Q_{\mu\nu}({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h,{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h)+G_{\mu\nu}(h)({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h,{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h)

\displaystyle+O\big(({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)^{2}\big)+O\big(A^{2}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A\big)+O(A^{4})

\displaystyle+O\big(h\cdot({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)^{2}\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{2}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{4}\big)\,.

As stated previously, Lindblad and Rodnianski showed in Proposition 3.1 in
[
39
]
, that

\displaystyle P(\partial_{\mu}h,\partial_{\nu}h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}m^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\partial_{\mu}h_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\,m^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\partial_{\nu}h_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}m^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}m^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\partial_{\mu}h_{\alpha\beta}\,\partial_{\nu}h_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h_{{\alpha}}^{\;\;\;{\alpha}}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}h_{{\beta}}^{\;\;\;{\beta}}-\frac{1}{2}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h_{\alpha\beta}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}h^{{\alpha}{\beta}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h)^{2})\,,

and

\displaystyle Q_{\mu\nu}(\partial h,\partial h)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}h_{\beta\mu}\,\,m^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}m^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\partial_{\alpha^{\prime}}h_{\beta^{\prime}\nu}-m^{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}m^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\big(\partial_{\alpha}h_{\beta\mu}\,\,\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}h_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}-\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}h_{\beta\mu}\,\,\partial_{\alpha}h_{\alpha^{\prime}\nu}\big)

\displaystyle+m^{{\alpha}{\alpha}^{\prime}}m^{{\beta}{\beta}^{\prime}}\big(\partial_{\mu}h_{{\alpha}^{\prime}{\beta}^{\prime}}\,\partial_{\alpha}h_{{\beta}\nu}-\partial_{\alpha}h_{{\alpha}^{\prime}{\beta}^{\prime}}\,\partial_{\mu}h_{{\beta}\nu}\big)+m^{{\alpha}{\alpha}^{\prime}}m^{{\beta}{\beta}^{\prime}}\big(\partial_{\nu}h_{{\alpha}^{\prime}{\beta}^{\prime}}\,\partial_{\alpha}h_{{\beta}\mu}-\partial_{\alpha}h_{{\alpha}^{\prime}{\beta}^{\prime}}\,\partial_{\nu}h_{{\beta}\mu}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}m^{{\alpha}{\alpha}^{\prime}}m^{{\beta}{\beta}^{\prime}}\big(\partial_{{\beta}^{\prime}}h_{{\alpha}{\alpha}^{\prime}}\,\partial_{\mu}h_{{\beta}\nu}-\partial_{\mu}h_{{\alpha}{\alpha}^{\prime}}\,\partial_{{\beta}^{\prime}}h_{{\beta}\nu}\big)+\frac{1}{2}m^{{\alpha}{\alpha}^{\prime}}m^{{\beta}{\beta}^{\prime}}\big(\partial_{{\beta}^{\prime}}h_{{\alpha}{\alpha}^{\prime}}\,\partial_{\nu}h_{{\beta}\mu}-\partial_{\nu}h_{{\alpha}{\alpha}^{\prime}}\,\partial_{{\beta}^{\prime}}h_{{\beta}\mu}\big)\,,

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}h_{\beta\mu}\,\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\alpha}}h^{{\beta}}_{\;\;\;\nu}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}h_{\beta\mu}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}h^{{\alpha}}_{\;\;\;\nu}+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}h_{{\beta}\mu}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}h^{{\alpha}}_{\;\;\;\nu}

\displaystyle+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h^{{\alpha}{\beta}}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}h_{{\beta}\nu}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}h^{{\alpha}{\beta}}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h_{{\beta}\nu}

\displaystyle+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}h^{{\alpha}{\beta}}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}h_{{\beta}\mu}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}h^{{\alpha}{\beta}}\,{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}h_{{\beta}\mu}

\displaystyle+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}h_{{\alpha}}^{\;\;\;{\alpha}}\,{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h_{{\beta}\nu}-\frac{1}{2}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h_{{\alpha}}^{\;\;\;{\alpha}}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}h_{{\beta}\nu}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}h_{{\alpha}}^{\;\;\;{\alpha}}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}h_{{\beta}\mu}-\frac{1}{2}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}h_{{\alpha}}^{\;\;\;{\alpha}}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}h_{{\beta}\mu}\,,

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h)^{2})\,,

and

\displaystyle G_{\mu\nu}(h)(\partial h,\partial h)=O(h\cdot({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h)^{2})\,.

(11.3)

Thus,

\displaystyle g^{\alpha\beta}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\beta}h_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h)^{2})+O(h\cdot({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h)^{2})

\displaystyle+O\big(({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)^{2}\big)+O\big(A^{2}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A\big)+O(A^{4})

\displaystyle+O\big(h\cdot({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A)^{2}\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{2}\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}A\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{4}\big)\,.

Differentiating the equation above and using the fact that the
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}
commutes with
{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}
, we obtain the result.

12.
Using the bootstrap assumption to exhibit the structure of the source terms of the Einstein-Yang-Mills system

12.1.
Using the bootstrap assumption to exhibit the structure of the source terms for the Yang-Mills potential

Now, we want to use the bootstrap assumption to exhibit the structure of the source term for the wave equation on the Yang-Mills potential in the Lorenz gauge and in wave coordinates, depending also on the space-dimension
n
.

In fact, we would like to estimate the term
\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}|g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)|\,w\cdot dx_{1}\ldots dx_{n}d\tau
.
Using the inequality
a\cdot b\lesssim a^{2}+b^{2}
, we get,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}\sqrt{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}}\sqrt{w}\cdot|g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)|\,\sqrt{w}\cdot dx_{1}\ldots dx_{n}d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}\frac{|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)|^{2}}{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}}\cdot dx_{1}\ldots dx_{n}d\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)|^{2}\,w\cdot dx_{1}\ldots dx_{n}d\tau\;

where one can choose
\lambda>0
so that
\int\frac{1}{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}}d\tau
is integrable.

Yet, we have

\displaystyle|g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|

\displaystyle+|g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|\,,

and consequently, we have

\displaystyle(1+\tau)\cdot|g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\tau)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+\tau)\cdot|g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}\,.

Lemma 12.1
.

We have

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta+1+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}},\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+1+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)\;.

Proof.

We have already estimated

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|)+O(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|)\;\big)\,.

Yet, we can now look at each term one by one.

Terms of the type
|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|
:

We have

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor,\;|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|J|\leq|I|,\;|K|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|\;.

However, for
|J|,\;|K|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor
, based on what we have proved in Lemma
10.1
, we have

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|\lesssim E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Thus, we could write

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|

(12.1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|\Big)\;.

Terms of the type
|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|
:

Similarly,

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor,\;|K|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor,\;|L|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|J|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor,\;|K|\leq|I|,\;|L|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|J|\leq|I|,\;|K|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor,\;|L|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\;.

Again using that
E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\leq 1
, and
\epsilon\leq 1
, so that
E^{2}\cdot\epsilon^{2}\leq E\cdot\epsilon
, we have based on what we showed in Lemma
10.1
, that for
|J|,\;|K|,\;|L|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor
,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{-1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;,

and

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{-1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\;.

Terms of the type
|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|
:

Similarly,

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|\;.

Terms of the type
|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|
:

Also,

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{-1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\;

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\;.

Terms of the type
|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|
:

We have,

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)^{2}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|\;.

Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|\,.

Terms of the type
|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|
:

We have,

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{-1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{-1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{-1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)^{3}

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\;.

Hence,

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}(1+|q|)^{3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\;.

Terms of the type
|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|
:

Also,

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L||\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|\Big)\;.

Terms of the type
|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|
:

We have,

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}(1+|q|)^{3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h\Big)\;.

The whole term
|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|
:

Putting all together, we get

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}(1+|q|)^{3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}(1+|q|)^{3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}(1+|q|)^{3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3(n-1)}{2}-3\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)\;.

12.2.
Using the bootstrap assumption to exhibit the structure of the source terms for the metric

We aim to use the bootstrap assumption to exhibit the structure of the source term for the wave equation on the metric in wave coordinates coupled to the Yang-Mills potential in the Lorenz gauge, depending on the space-dimension
n
.

Recall that the weighted energy for
h^{0}
is in fact infinite; thus, the energy was defined for
h^{1}=h-h^{0}
. Thus, the equation that we are interested in, is the wave equation for
h^{1}
. We have for all
U,V\in\{\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{\mu}}\,\,|\,\,\mu\in\{0,1,\ldots,n\}\}
,

\displaystyle g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h^{1}_{UV}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h_{UV}-g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h^{0}_{UV}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle P(\partial_{\mu}h,\partial_{\nu}h)+Q_{\mu\nu}(\partial h,\partial h)+G_{\mu\nu}(h)(\partial h,\partial h)

\displaystyle-4<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{\mu},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A_{\nu}>

\displaystyle+m_{\mu\nu}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{{\alpha}},{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A^{{\alpha}}>

\displaystyle-4\cdot\big(<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{\mu},[A_{\nu},A^{{\beta}}]>+<[A_{\mu},A_{{\beta}}],{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\nu}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A_{\nu}>\big)

\displaystyle+m_{\mu\nu}\cdot\big(<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}A_{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A_{{\alpha}},[A^{{\alpha}},A^{{\beta}}]>+<[A_{{\alpha}},A_{{\beta}}],{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\alpha}}A^{{\beta}}-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}^{{\beta}}A^{{\alpha}}>\big)

\displaystyle-4<[A_{\mu},A_{{\beta}}],[A_{\nu},A^{{\beta}}]>+m_{\mu\nu}\cdot<[A_{{\alpha}},A_{{\beta}}],[A^{{\alpha}},A^{{\beta}}]>

\displaystyle+O\big(h\cdot(\partial A)^{2}\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{2}\cdot\partial A\big)+O\big(h\cdot A^{4}\big)\,

\displaystyle-g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h^{0}_{UV}\;.

Thus, for all
U,V\in\{\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{\mu}}\,\,|\,\,\mu\in\{0,1,\ldots,n\}\}
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h^{1}_{UV})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}h))

\displaystyle+O({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A))

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A))+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{N}}A)\;\big)\,

\displaystyle+O({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h^{0}))\;.

To estimate the term
\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}|g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{1}))|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{1}))|\,w\cdot dx_{1}\ldots dx_{n}d\tau
, we use the inequality
a\cdot b\lesssim a^{2}+b^{2}
, to write

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}\sqrt{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}}\sqrt{w}\cdot|g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{1}))|\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{1}))|\,\sqrt{w}\cdot dx_{1}\ldots dx_{n}d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}\frac{|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{1}))|^{2}}{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}}\cdot dx_{1}\ldots dx_{n}d\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{1}))|^{2}\,w\cdot dx_{1}\ldots dx_{n}d\tau\;.

However, we have

\displaystyle g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)-g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{0})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)+g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)

\displaystyle-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{0})\,.

Using the triangular inequality, we have

\displaystyle|g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{1})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|+|g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|

\displaystyle+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h^{0})|\,,

and therefore, we get

\displaystyle(1+\tau)\cdot|g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{1})|^{2}

(12.9)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\tau)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+\tau)\cdot|g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+\tau)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h^{0})|^{2}\,.

We would like to estimate the term
|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|
.

Lemma 12.2
.

We have

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+1+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)
