\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta+1+3\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)\;.

Proof.

We already showed in Lemma
11.3
, that

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|+|L|+|M|\leq|I|}\big(\;O(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}h)|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A)|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A)|)

\displaystyle+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A)|)+O(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{L}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{M}}A|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{N}}A|)\;\big)\,.

Terms of the type
|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|
:

We have

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|+|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|

Proof.

.

Proof.

We have

12.3.
The source terms for
n\geq 5

Lemma 12.5
.

For
n\geq 5
,
\epsilon\leq 1
, we have

\displaystyle(1+t)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}\,

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{-1}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

Proof.

For
n\geq 5
, we examine one by one the terms in
(1+t)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}
. We get

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{6+2(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{4+2(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{-1}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

And,

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{6+2(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{4+2(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{10+4(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{8-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{6+2(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{8+4(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{3}}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{8-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}\Big)

(where we used the fact that
\gamma\geq\delta
).

And,

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{4+2(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{6+2(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}},\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{8+4(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{3}}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{8-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}\Big)

(using the fact that
\gamma\geq\delta
).

Also,

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{4+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{2}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{6+2(\gamma-\delta)+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{4+2(\gamma-\delta)+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{3}}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

Lemma 12.6
.

For
n\geq 5
,

\displaystyle(1+t)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

Proof.

For
n\geq 5
, we examine the terms in
(1+t)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|^{2}
, one by one. We have

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{4+2(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{6+2(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{8+4(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{3}}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{8-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

And,

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{4+2(\gamma-\delta)+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{3}}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{6+2(\gamma-\delta)+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{8+4(\gamma-\delta)+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{4}}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{8-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

And,

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{6+2(\gamma-\delta)+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

Also,

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\Big(\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{4+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{2}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{6+2(\gamma-\delta)+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{4-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{8+4(\gamma-\delta)+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{4}}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{8-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{4+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

13.
Energy estimates

Definition 13.1
.

We define
\widehat{w}
by

\displaystyle\widehat{w}(q)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
(1+|q|)^{2\mu}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}(1+q)^{1+2\gamma}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
(1-q)^{2\mu}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

for
\gamma>0
and
\mu<0
. Note that the definition of
\widehat{w}
, is so that on one hand, for
\gamma\neq-\frac{1}{2}
and
\mu\neq 0
(which is assumed here), we would have

\displaystyle\widehat{w}^{\prime}(q)\sim\frac{\widehat{w}(q)}{(1+|q|)}\;,

(see Lemma
16.1
). On the other hand, we want that for
q<0
, the derivative
\frac{\partial\widehat{w}}{\partial q}
to be non-vanishing.

Remark 13.1
.

We take
\mu<0
(instead of
\mu>0
), because we want the derivative
\frac{\partial\widehat{w}}{\partial q}>0
, as we will see that this is what we need in order to obtain an energy estimate on the fields (see Lemma
13.4
). In other words,
\mu<0
is a necessary condition to ensure that
\widehat{w}^{\prime}(q)
enters with the right sign in the energy estimate.

Definition 13.2
.

We define
\widetilde{w}
by

\displaystyle\widetilde{w}(q)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\widehat{w}(q)+w(q)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}2(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
1+(1+|q|)^{2\mu}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Note that the definition of
\widetilde{w}
is constructed so that Lemma
13.1
holds.

Lemma 13.1
.

We have

\displaystyle\widetilde{w}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\widehat{w}^{\prime}\;.

Furthermore, for
\mu<0
, we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{w}(q)

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle w(q)\;.

Proof.

We compute the derivative with respect to
q
,

\displaystyle\widetilde{w}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widehat{w}^{\prime}(q)+w^{\prime}(q)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}2\cdot\widehat{w}^{\prime}(q)\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\widehat{w}^{\prime}(q)\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Consequently,

\displaystyle\widetilde{w}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\widehat{w}^{\prime}\;.

Now, on one hand, since
\widehat{w}\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{w}(q)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle w(q)\;.

On the other hand, since
\mu<0
, we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{w}(q)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}2(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
1+(1+|q|)^{2\mu}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}2(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
2\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2w(q)

Thus

\displaystyle\widetilde{w}(q)

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle w(q)\;.

Definition 13.3
.

Let
\Phi
be a tensor of any order, say a 2-tensor
\Phi_{\mu\nu}
, either valued in the Lie algebra
{\cal G}
, or a a scalar. For any
{\alpha}\,,\,{\beta}\in\{r,t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n}\}
, we define the following scalar product by

\displaystyle<\partial_{\alpha}\Phi,\partial_{\beta}\Phi>

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mu,\,\nu\in\{t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n}\}}<\partial_{\alpha}\Phi_{\mu\nu},\partial_{\beta}\Phi_{\mu\nu}>\;.

Lemma 13.2
.

Let
\Phi_{\mu\nu}
be a tensor solution of the following tensorial wave equation

\displaystyle g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}\Phi_{\mu\nu}=S_{\mu\nu}\,,

(13.2)

where
S_{\mu\nu}
is the source term, with a sufficiently smooth metric
g
. Assume that the field is decaying fast enough at spatial infinity for all time
t
, such that in wave coordinates
\{t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n} \}
, we have for
j
running over spatial indices
\{x^{1},\ldots,x^{n} \}
,

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{rj}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;,

(13.3)

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{tr}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;.

(13.4)

Then, we have the following

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-(m^{tt}+H^{tt})<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(m^{ij}+H^{ij})<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}\Big(-(m^{tt}+H^{tt})<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(m^{ij}+H^{ij})<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}-2<\partial_{t}\Phi,S>\cdot w

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big((-\partial_{t}H^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(\partial_{t}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle-2(\partial_{i}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>-2\partial_{j}(H^{tj})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>)\Big)\cdot w

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2H^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{i}}{r})-2H^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{j}}{r})

\displaystyle+H^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-H^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(<\partial_{t}\Phi+\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi+\partial_{r}\Phi>+\delta^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi-\frac{x_{i}}{r}\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi-\frac{x_{j}}{r}\partial_{r}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\;,

where the integration on
\Sigma_{t}
is taken with respect to the measure
dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}
, and the integration in
t
is taken with respect to the measure
dt
and where the scalar product is taken as in Definition
13.3
.

Proof.

Let
d^{n}x:=dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}
and let
w^{\prime}(q):=\frac{\partial}{\partial q}w(q)
. We denote by
i
and
j
, spatial indices running only over
\{1,2,\ldots,n\}
. We compute, on one hand,

\displaystyle\frac{\text{d}}{dt}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}<\partial_{\alpha}\Phi,\partial_{\beta}\Phi>-2g^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-2g^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\text{d}}{dt}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-g^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+g^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

(using the symmetry of metric
g
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big((-\partial_{t}g^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(\partial_{t}g^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2g^{tt}<\partial^{2}_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+2g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-g^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+g^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot(\frac{\partial}{\partial q}w(q))\cdot(\partial_{t}q)\cdot d^{n}x

(using again the symmetry of metric
g
)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}

(where
I_{1},\;I_{2},\;I_{3}
are defined respectively as the last three integrals).

On the other hand, since we would like to get rid of the second order derivatives, or express them in terms of
g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}\Phi=S
, we compute independently,

\displaystyle<\partial_{t}\Phi,S>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle<\partial_{t}\Phi,g^{tt}\partial^{2}_{t}\Phi+g^{ij}\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\Phi+2g^{tj}\partial_{t}\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial^{2}_{t}\Phi>+g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\Phi>+2g^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\partial_{j}\Phi>\;.

In order to write
I_{2}
in that form, we integrate by parts,

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2g^{tt}<\partial^{2}_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+2g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2g^{tt}<\partial^{2}_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-2g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\Phi>-2(\partial_{i}g^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot(\partial_{i}q)\cdot d^{n}x+2\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{rj}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}

(where
d\sigma^{n-1}
is the volume form on the unit
(n-1)
-sphere)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2g^{tt}<\partial^{2}_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-2g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\Phi>-4g^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle-2(\partial_{i}g^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>+4g^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot(\partial_{i}q)\cdot d^{n}x

(where we used the fact that the boundary terms vanish)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}-2<\partial_{t}\Phi,S>\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2(\partial_{i}g^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>+4g^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot(\partial_{i}q)\cdot d^{n}x\;.

However, we notice that

\displaystyle 2<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\partial_{j}\Phi>=\partial_{j}(<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>)\;.

Thus, integrating by parts using the fact the that the boundary term vanishes, i.e.

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{tr}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;,

since the fields are decaying fast at spatial infinity, we obtain

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}-2<\partial_{t}\Phi,S>\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2(\partial_{i}g^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>+2g^{tj}\partial_{j}(<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>)\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot(\partial_{i}q)\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}-2<\partial_{t}\Phi,S>\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2(\partial_{i}g^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>-2\partial_{j}(g^{tj})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>)\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\partial_{i}q)-2g^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\partial_{j}q)\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x\;.

Putting together, we get

\displaystyle\frac{\text{d}}{dt}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-g^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+g^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}-2<\partial_{t}\Phi,S>\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big((-\partial_{t}g^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(\partial_{t}g^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle-2(\partial_{i}g^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>-2\partial_{j}(g^{tj})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>)\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\partial_{i}q)-2g^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\partial_{j}q)

\displaystyle-g^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\partial_{t}q)+g^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\partial_{t}q)\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x\,.

Since
q=r-t
, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}q=-1\;,

and

\displaystyle\partial_{j}q=\partial_{j}r=\partial_{j}\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{n}(x_{k})^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{x_{j}}{r}\,

and since be definition

\displaystyle H^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g^{\mu\nu}-m^{\mu\nu}\;,

and therefore, for all
{\alpha}\in\{\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{\mu}}\;,\;\mu\in\{0,1,\ldots,n\}\}

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}H^{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{\alpha}g^{\mu\nu}-\partial_{a}m^{\mu\nu}=\partial_{\alpha}g^{\mu\nu}\;,

we get

\displaystyle\frac{\text{d}}{dt}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-g^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+g^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}-2<\partial_{t}\Phi,S>\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big((-\partial_{t}H^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(\partial_{t}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle-2(\partial_{i}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>-2\partial_{j}(H^{tj})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>)\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2g^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{i}}{r})-2g^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{j}}{r})

\displaystyle+g^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-g^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x\;.

Thus,

\displaystyle\frac{\text{d}}{dt}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-g^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+g^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}-2<\partial_{t}\Phi,S>\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big((-\partial_{t}H^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(\partial_{t}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle-2(\partial_{i}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>-2\partial_{j}(H^{tj})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>)\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2(g^{ij}-m^{ij})<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{i}}{r})-2(g^{tj}-m^{tj})<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{j}}{r})

\displaystyle+(g^{tt}-m^{tt})<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-(g^{ij}-m^{ij})<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2m^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{i}}{r})-2m^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{j}}{r})

\displaystyle+m^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-m^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x\;.

Now, we would like to compute,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2m^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{i}}{r})-2m^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{j}}{r})

\displaystyle+m^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-m^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x^{j}}{r})+0-<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-\delta^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x\;.

However,

\displaystyle\partial_{r}=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\partial_{j}r\cdot\partial_{j}=m^{ij}\partial_{i}r\cdot\partial_{j}=\frac{x^{j}}{r}\cdot\partial_{j}\;.

Thus,

\displaystyle\partial_{r}\Phi=\frac{x^{j}}{r}\cdot\partial_{j}\Phi\;.

We consider the derivatives restricted on the
n
-spheres

\displaystyle\partial_{i}-E(\partial_{i},\partial_{r})\cdot\partial_{r}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{i}-E(\partial_{i},\frac{x^{j}}{r}\partial_{j})\cdot\partial_{r}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{i}-\frac{x_{i}}{r}\partial_{r}\,.

We have

\displaystyle\delta^{ij}<(\partial_{i}-\frac{x_{i}}{r}\partial_{r})\Phi,(\partial_{j}-\frac{x_{j}}{r}\partial_{r})\Phi>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>-2\delta^{ij}<\frac{x_{i}}{r}\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>+\delta^{ij}\frac{x_{i}}{r}\frac{x_{j}}{r}<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>-2<\frac{x^{j}}{r}\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>+\frac{r^{2}}{r^{2}}<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>-2<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>+<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>\;.

Hence,

\displaystyle\delta^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>=\delta^{ij}<(\partial_{i}-\frac{x_{i}}{r}\partial_{r})\Phi,(\partial_{j}-\frac{x_{j}}{r}\partial_{r})\Phi>+<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>\;.

Injecting, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2m^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{i}}{r})-2m^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{j}}{r})

\displaystyle+m^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-m^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>-<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>

\displaystyle-\delta^{ij}<(\partial_{i}-\frac{x_{i}}{r}\partial_{r})\Phi,(\partial_{j}-\frac{x_{j}}{r}\partial_{r})\Phi>-<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-<\partial_{t}\Phi+\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi+\partial_{r}\Phi>-\delta^{ij}<(\partial_{i}-\frac{x_{i}}{r}\partial_{r})\Phi,(\partial_{j}-\frac{x_{j}}{r}\partial_{r})\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x\;.

As a result, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\text{d}}{dt}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-g^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+g^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}-2<\partial_{t}\Phi,S>\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big((-\partial_{t}H^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(\partial_{t}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle-2(\partial_{i}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>-2\partial_{j}(H^{tj})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>)\Big)\cdot w\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2H^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{i}}{r})-2H^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{j}}{r})

\displaystyle+H^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-H^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(<\partial_{t}\Phi+\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi+\partial_{r}\Phi>+\delta^{ij}<(\partial_{i}-\frac{x_{i}}{r}\partial_{r})\Phi,(\partial_{j}-\frac{x_{j}}{r}\partial_{r})\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\cdot d^{n}x\;.

Integrating in time
t
, we obtain the result.

Lemma 13.3
.

Assume that the perturbation of the Minkowski metric is such that
H^{\mu\nu}=g^{\mu\nu}-m^{\mu\nu}
is bounded by a constant
C<\frac{1}{n}
, i.e.

\displaystyle|H|\leq C<\frac{1}{n}\;,

(13.6)

then we have

\displaystyle|\partial\Phi|^{2}\sim-(m^{tt}+H^{tt})<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(m^{ij}+H^{ij})<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\;,

where the scalar product of the partial derivatives is as in Definition
13.3
.

Remark 13.2
.

The assumption on
H
in
13.6
is satisfied under the bootstrap argument for initial data small enough.

Proof.

For each
\mu,\nu\in\{t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n}\}
, we have

\displaystyle H^{\mu\nu}\leq|H|=\Big(E_{\lambda\sigma}E_{{\alpha}{\beta}}H^{\lambda{\alpha}}H^{\sigma{\beta}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C

and therefore

\displaystyle-(m^{tt}+H^{tt})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(-1+H^{tt})=1+H^{tt}\leq 1+C

\displaystyle-(m^{tt}+H^{tt})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-C

and

\displaystyle(m^{ij}+H^{ij})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{ij}+C \;,

\displaystyle(m^{ij}+H^{ij})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\delta^{ij}-C \;.

Now, let
C^{ij}=C
for all
i,j
spatial indices. We get

\displaystyle(1-C)\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(\delta^{ij}-C^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-(m^{tt}+H^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(m^{ij}+H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+C)\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(\delta^{ij}+C^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\;.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle(1-C)\cdot|\partial\Phi|^{2}-C\cdot\sum_{i\neq j}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-(m^{tt}+H^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(m^{ij}+H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+C)\cdot|\partial\Phi|^{2}+C\cdot\sum_{i\neq j}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\;.

Using
|a|\cdot|b|\leq\frac{1}{2}a^{2}+\frac{1}{2}b^{2}
, we get

\displaystyle|<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{i}\Phi>|^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot|<\partial_{j}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>|^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{i}\Phi>+\frac{1}{2}\cdot<\partial_{j}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\;,

and therefore, we have

\displaystyle C\cdot\sum_{i,j,\;i\neq j}|<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{2}\cdot\sum_{i,j,\;i\neq j}\Big(<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{i}\Phi>+<\partial_{j}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{2}\cdot\Big(2(n-1)\cdot|\partial\Phi|^{2}\Big)

(where we used, in counting the sum, the fact that
\sum_{i=1}^{n}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{i}\Phi>\leq|\partial\Phi|^{2}
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\cdot(n-1)\cdot|\partial\Phi|^{2}\;.

As a result,

\displaystyle(1-C)\cdot|\partial\Phi|^{2}-C\cdot(n-1)\cdot|\partial\Phi|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-(m^{tt}+H^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(m^{ij}+H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+C)\cdot|\partial\Phi|^{2}+C\cdot(n-1)\cdot|\partial\Phi|^{2}\;.

Consequently,

\displaystyle(1-C\cdot n))\cdot|\partial\Phi|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-(m^{tt}+H^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(m^{ij}+H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+C\cdot n)\cdot|\partial\Phi|^{2}\;.

Lemma 13.4
.

Let
\Phi_{\mu\nu}
be a tensor solution of the following tensorial wave equation

\displaystyle g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}\Phi_{\mu\nu}=S_{\mu\nu}\,,

(13.7)

where
S_{\mu\nu}
is the source term, with a sufficiently smooth metric
g
. Assume that
H^{\mu\nu}=g^{\mu\nu}-m^{\mu\nu}
satisfies

\displaystyle|H|\leq C<\frac{1}{n}\;,\quad\text{where $n$ is the space dimension},

and assume that the field
\Phi
is decaying fast enough at spatial infinity for all time
t
, such that in wave coordinates
\{t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n} \}
, we have for
j
running over spatial indices
\{x^{1},\ldots,x^{n} \}
,

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{rj}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0

(13.8)

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{tr}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;.

(13.9)

Then, we have the following

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|\partial\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}|\partial\Phi|^{2}\cdot w+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}-2<\partial_{t}\Phi,S>\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot|w^{\prime}(q)|

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(|\partial_{t}\Phi+\partial_{r}\Phi|^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|(\partial_{i}-\frac{x_{i}}{r}\partial_{r})\Phi|^{2}\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\;.

where the integration on
\Sigma_{t}
is taken with respect to the measure
dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}
, and the integration in
t
is taken with respect to the measure
dt
.

Proof.

We examine the term

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big((-\partial_{t}H^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(\partial_{t}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

\displaystyle-2(\partial_{i}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>-2\partial_{j}(H^{tj})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>)\Big)\cdot w\;.

Given the definition of the norms computed in wave coordinates
\{t,x^{i}\quad i\in\{1,\ldots,n\}\}
,

\displaystyle|(-\partial_{t}H^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}H^{tt}|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi|^{2}\leq|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\;,

\displaystyle|(\partial_{t}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}H^{ij}|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{i}\Phi|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{j}\Phi|\leq|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\;,

\displaystyle|(\partial_{i}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{i}H^{ij}|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{j}\Phi|\leq|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\;,

\displaystyle|\partial_{j}(H^{tj})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{j}H^{tj}|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi|^{2}\leq|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\;.

Consequently, we get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big((-\partial_{t}H^{tt})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>+(\partial_{t}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>

(13.10)

\displaystyle-2(\partial_{i}H^{ij})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>-2\partial_{j}(H^{tj})\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>)\Big)\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot|w|\;.

We look at the term

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2H^{ij}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{i}}{r})-2H^{tj}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{j}}{r})

\displaystyle+H^{tt}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-H^{ij}\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\;.

Using the fact that
|x_{i}|\leq r
for all
i
, spatial index, we get

\displaystyle|H^{ij}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{i}}{r})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|H^{ij}|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{j}\Phi|\cdot\frac{|x_{i}|}{r}\leq|H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\;,

\displaystyle|H^{tj}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{j}}{r})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\;,

\displaystyle|H^{tt}<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\;,

\displaystyle|H^{ij}<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\;.

Thus,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(-2H^{ij}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{i}}{r})-2H^{tj}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot(\frac{x_{j}}{r})

(13.11)

\displaystyle+H^{tt}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>-H^{ij}\cdot<\partial_{i}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot|w^{\prime}(q)|\;.

Using what we showed in Lemma
13.2
and injecting the estimates (
13.10
) and (
13.11
) that we proved, and using Lemma
13.3
, we get the result.

14.
A Hardy type inequality

We will prove a Hardy type inequality with the weight
w
that we defined in Definition
9.2
. However, since we will need a Hardy type inequality for a more general weight for the case of lower space-dimensions (which we will treat papers that follow), we will prove a Hardy type inequality for a more general weight
\widehat{w}
which we will define in what follows (the weight
w
corresponds to the case of
\mu=0
in
\widehat{w}
).

Definition 14.1
.

We define
\widehat{w}
by

\displaystyle\widehat{w}(q)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
(1+|q|)^{2\mu}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}(1+q)^{1+2\gamma}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
(1-q)^{2\mu}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

for
\gamma>0
being the same as in Definition
9.2
, and for
\mu\in{\mathbb{R}}
which could be restricted later in paper the follow for the lower dimensions. In other words, we will finally take in this paper
\mu=0
, however we will perform our calculations with a general
\mu\neq\frac{1}{2}
as will be pointed out later when it is needed.

Lemma 14.1
.

Let
\widehat{w}
defined as in Definition
14.1
.
Let
\Phi
a tensor that decays fast enough at spatial infinity for all time
t
, such that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}\cdot(1+|q|)}\widehat{w}(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;.

(14.1)

Let
R(\Omega)\geq 0
, be a function of
\Omega\in{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}
. Then, for
\gamma\neq 0
and
\mu\neq\frac{1}{2}
,
0\leq a\leq n-1
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot\frac{\widehat{w}(q)}{(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\cdot dr\cdot d\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma,\mu)\cdot\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot\widehat{w}(q)\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>\cdot dr\cdot d\sigma^{n-1}\;,

where the constant
c(\gamma,\mu)
does not depend on
R(\Omega)
.

Proof.

Let

\displaystyle m(q)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{w}(q)}{(1+|q|)}=\begin{cases}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0\;,\\
(1+|q|)^{2\mu-1}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0\;,\end{cases}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}(1+q)^{2\gamma}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0\;,\\
(1-q)^{2\mu-1}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0\;,\end{cases}

and we compute,

\displaystyle m^{\prime}(q):=\begin{cases}2\gamma(1+|q|)^{2\gamma-1}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0\;,\\
(1-2\mu)(1+|q|)^{2\mu-1}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0\;.\end{cases}

We want to prove the following Hardy type inequality for
a
, such that
0\leq a\leq n-1
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{\infty}\frac{|\phi|^{2}}{(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot\frac{\widehat{w}(q)\,r^{n-1}\cdot dr\cdot d\sigma^{n-1}}{(1+t+|q|)^{a}}\lesssim\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{\infty}|\partial\phi|^{2}\,\frac{\widehat{w}(q)\,r^{n-1}\cdot dr\cdot d\sigma^{n-1}}{(1+t+|q|)^{a}}

which means that we need to prove that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{\infty}\frac{|\phi|^{2}}{(1+|q|)}\cdot\frac{m(q)\,r^{n-1}\cdot dr\cdot d\sigma^{n-1}}{(1+t+|q|)^{a}}\lesssim\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{\infty}|\partial\phi|^{2}\,\frac{(1+|q|)\cdot m(q)\,r^{n-1}\cdot dr\cdot d\sigma^{n-1}}{(1+t+|q|)^{a}}\;.

Since the term
\frac{(R(\Omega))^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>
is non-negative, we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\partial_{r}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)drd\sigma^{n-1}

(14.3)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}-\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\Big(\frac{(R(\Omega))^{n-1}}{(1+t+R(\Omega))^{a}}m(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}\;.

We assume that
\Phi
decays fast enough at spatial infinity for all time
t
, so that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}\cdot(1+|q|)}\widehat{w}(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(14.4)

and therefore

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=0}^{r=\infty}\partial_{r}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}\cdot(1+|q|)}\widehat{w}(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)drd\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 0\;.

We compute

\displaystyle\partial_{r}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot m(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{r}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\Big)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>+2\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\Phi>\;.

We evaluate the term

\displaystyle\partial_{r}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(n-1)r^{n-2}(1+t+r)^{-a}m(q)+r^{n-1}\cdot(-a)(1+t+r)^{-a-1}m(q)+\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m^{\prime}(q)\cdot(\partial_{r}q)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot\Big(\frac{(n-1)}{r}\cdot m(q)-\frac{a}{(1+t+r)}\cdot m(q)+m^{\prime}(q)\Big)

(since
q=r-t
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot\Big(m(q)\cdot(\frac{(n-1)}{r}-\frac{a}{(1+t+r)})+m^{\prime}(q)\Big)\;.

Since
-\frac{a}{(1+t+r)}\geq-\frac{a}{r}
, and since
(n-1)-a\geq 0
, we get

\displaystyle\partial_{r}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\Big)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot\Big(m(q)\cdot\frac{\big((n-1)-a\big)}{r}+m^{\prime}(q)\Big)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot m^{\prime}(q)\;.

Therefore, using also that
<\Phi,\Phi>\geq 0
, we get

\displaystyle\partial_{r}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{r}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\Big)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>+2\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\Phi>

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot m^{\prime}(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>+2\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\Phi>\;.

By integrating and using the fact that the integral of the left hand side of the above inequality is non-positive, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot m^{\prime}(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>drd\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{-2r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\Phi>drd\sigma^{n-1}\;.

Using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and the fact that
m^{\prime}(q)\neq 0
for all
q
(since
\gamma\neq 0
and
\mu\neq\frac{1}{2}
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{-2r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m(q)\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\Phi>drd\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\sqrt{\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}}\sqrt{m^{\prime}(q)}\cdot\sqrt{<\Phi,\Phi>}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}}\frac{m(q)}{\sqrt{m^{\prime}(q)}}\sqrt{<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>}drd\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\Big(\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}m^{\prime}(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>drd\sigma^{n-1}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\frac{(m(q))^{2}}{m^{\prime}(q)}\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>drd\sigma^{n-1}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\;.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot m^{\prime}(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>drd\sigma^{n-1}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\Big(\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\frac{(m(q))^{2}}{m^{\prime}(q)}\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>drd\sigma^{n-1}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\;.

We have

\displaystyle m^{\prime}(q)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}2\gamma(1+|q|)^{2\gamma-1}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
(1-2\mu)(1+|q|)^{2\mu-1}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}2\gamma\frac{m(q)}{(1+|q|)}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
(1-2\mu)\frac{m(q)}{(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Thus,

\displaystyle\min\{2\gamma,(1-2\mu)\}\cdot\frac{m(q)}{(1+|q|)}\leq m^{\prime}(q)\leq\max\{2\gamma,(1-2\mu)\}\cdot\frac{m(q)}{(1+|q|)}\;.

For
\gamma\neq 0
and
\mu\neq\frac{1}{2}
, we have
\min\{2\gamma,(1-2\mu)\}\neq 0
and
\max\{2\gamma,(1-2\mu)\}\neq 0
, and therefore, we get

\displaystyle m^{\prime}(q)\sim\frac{m(q)}{(1+|q|)}\;.

(14.7)

As a result,

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot\frac{m(q)}{(1+|q|)}\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>drd\sigma^{n-1}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma,\mu)\cdot\Big(\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot(1+|q|)\cdot m(q)\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>drd\sigma^{n-1}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\;.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot\frac{\widehat{w}(q)}{(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>drd\sigma^{n-1}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma,\mu)\cdot\Big(\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot\widehat{w}(q)\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>drd\sigma^{n-1}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\;.

Corollary 14.1
.

Let
w
defined as in Definition
9.2
, where
\gamma>0
.
Let
\Phi
a tensor that decays fast enough at spatial infinity for all time
t
, such that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;.

(14.8)

Let
R(\Omega)\geq 0
, be a function of
\Omega\in{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}
. Then, since
\gamma\neq 0
, we have for
0\leq a\leq n-1
, that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot\frac{w(q)}{(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\cdot dr\cdot d\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=R(\Omega)}^{r=\infty}\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot w(q)\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>\cdot dr\cdot d\sigma^{n-1}\;,

where the constant
c(\gamma)
does not depend on
R(\Omega)
.

Proof.

By taking in Lemma
14.1
, on one hand
\mu=0
(which satisfies the assumption
\mu\neq\frac{1}{2}
) and and on the other hand
\gamma>0
, as considered in Definition
9.2
(which in particular satisfies the assumption
\gamma\neq 0
), we obtain the result.

15.
The commutator term for
n\geq 4

Lemma 15.1
.

We have for all
|I|
,
\delta\leq\frac{(n-2)}{2}
,
\epsilon\leq 1
,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}H(t,x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\begin{cases}E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

and

\displaystyle|H(t,x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}},\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Proof.

We showed in Lemma
10.4
, that

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h^{1}(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

However, for
n\geq 4
, we have
h=h^{1}
. In addition, we know from Lemma
5.1
, that

\displaystyle H^{\mu\nu}=-h^{\mu\nu}+O^{\mu\nu}(h^{2})\;.

Since for all
|I|
,

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}},\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

we get

\displaystyle|H(t,x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\Big(\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}}+O(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}})\Big),\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\Big(\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}+O(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}(1+|q|))\Big),\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\Big(\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+O(\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\frac{\epsilon\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}\Big),\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Thus,

\displaystyle|H(t,x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}},\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

However, given the fact that in the expression

\displaystyle H^{\mu\nu}=-h^{\mu\nu}+O^{\mu\nu}(h^{2})\;,

here the
O^{\mu\nu}(h^{2})
happen to be a product of tensors of
m
with
h^{2}
, we then also have that

\displaystyle{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}H^{\mu\nu}=-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}h^{\mu\nu}+O_{\alpha}^{\,\,\,\,\mu\nu}(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h)\;.

(15.2)

Since for all
|I|
,

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h(t,x)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

we obtain,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)(t,x)|+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h(t,x)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\Big(\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}}\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+2\gamma}}\Big),\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{(n-1)-2\delta}}\big)\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Thus, if
\epsilon\leq 1
and if
\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta\geq\frac{1}{2}
, which means if
\delta\leq\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\leq\frac{(n-2)}{2}
, we get

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)(t,x)|+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h(t,x)|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

which gives the result for
|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}H(t,x)|
.

Lemma 15.2
.

We have for all
|I|
,
\delta\leq\frac{(n-2)}{2}
,
\epsilon\leq 1
,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H)(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

and

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}},\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Proof.

We have already showed in Lemma
5.1
, that

\displaystyle H^{\mu\nu}=-h^{\mu\nu}+O^{\mu\nu}(h^{2}).

Hence, for
\mu,\nu\in\{x^{0},x^{1},\ldots,x^{n}\}
, we have

\displaystyle H_{\mu\nu}=-h_{\mu\nu}+O_{\mu\nu}(h^{2}).

Using again that here that
O_{\mu\nu}(h^{2})
and
O_{{\alpha}\mu\nu}(h\cdot\partial h)
are in fact product of Minkowski metric with
h
and
{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h
, and using Lemma
10.6
, as well as the Leibniz rule for Lie derivatives, we obtain that for all
Z\in\cal Z
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}H_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h_{\mu\nu}+O_{\mu\nu}(h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h)

\displaystyle{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}H)_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h)_{\mu\nu}+O_{{\alpha}\mu\nu}({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h)+O_{{\alpha}\mu\nu}(h\cdot{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}h)).

Since
|h|
and
|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h|
obey the same estimate, and since also
|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}h|
and
|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)|
obey the same estimate, we then derive the same estimate for
|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}H^{\mu\nu}|
as for
|H^{\mu\nu}|
and the same estimate for
|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z}H)^{\mu\nu}|
as for
|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}H^{\mu\nu}|
. By induction, we get the result for all
|I|
.

We now look at the commutator term for
n\geq 4
.

Lemma 15.3
.

For
\Phi=H
or
\Phi=A
, using the bootstrap assumption on
\Phi
, we have

\displaystyle|g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}\Phi)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|\Big)\cdot\begin{cases}C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+\gamma}},\quad\text{for }q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}},\,\quad\text{for }q<0,\end{cases}

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|\Big)

\displaystyle\times\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{for }q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}},\,\quad\text{for }q<0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|-1}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}\Phi|\;.

Proof.

Let
\Phi_{\mu\nu}
be a tensor valued either in the Lie algebra (which could be the one tensor Yang-Mills potential) or a two tensor valued a s a scalar (the two tensor of the metric
h^{1}
), satisfying the following tensorial wave equation

\displaystyle g^{\lambda{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}\Phi_{\mu\nu}=S_{\mu\nu}\,,

where
S_{\mu\nu}
is the source term. Based on a more refined estimate that we will prove in a paper that follows that deals with the case
n=3
, (see also
[
36
]
,
[
40
]
and
[
39
]
), we have

\displaystyle|g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}\Phi)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+t+|q|}\,\,\,\sum_{|K|\leq|I|,}\,\,\sum_{|J|+(|K|-1)_{+}\leq|I|}\,\,\,|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|\,\cdot{|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+|q|}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|,}\,\,\sum_{|J|+(|K|-1)_{+}\leq|I|}\!\!\!\!\!|({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H)_{LL}|\cdot{|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi|}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|-1}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}\Phi|\;,

where
(|K|-1)_{+}=|K|-1
if
|K|\geq 1
and
(|K|-1)_{+}=0
if

|K|=0
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle|g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}\Phi)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+|q|}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(\sum_{|J|+(|K|-1)_{+}\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|\Big)\cdot{|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|<|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}\Phi|\;.

Thus,

\displaystyle|g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}\Phi)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+|q|}\sum_{|K|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor}\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|\Big)\cdot{|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{1+|q|}\sum_{\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor\leq|K|\leq|I|}\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+1}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|\Big)\cdot{|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|-1}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}\Phi|\;.

Yet, for
|K|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor
, and for either
\Phi=H
or
\Phi=A
, using the bootstrap assumption, we obtain

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

and for
|J|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+1
,

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}},\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Consequently,

\displaystyle|g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}\Phi)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|\Big)\cdot\begin{cases}C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+\gamma}},\quad\text{for }q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}},\,\quad\text{for }q<0,\end{cases}

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|\Big)

\displaystyle\times\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{for }q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}},\,\quad\text{for }q<0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|-1}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}\Phi|\;.

Lemma 15.4
.

For
n\geq 4
,
\delta=0
,
\epsilon\leq 1
, for either
\Phi=H
or
\Phi=A
, using the bootstrap assumption on
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi
for
|K|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor
, we have

\displaystyle(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|^{2}\Big)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}(1+|q|)^{3}}

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|^{2}\Big)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\;.

Proof.

We showed in Lemma
15.3
, that

\displaystyle|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|\Big)\cdot\begin{cases}C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+\gamma}},\quad\text{for }q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}},\,\quad\text{for }q<0,\end{cases}

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|\Big)

\displaystyle\times\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{for }q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}},\,\quad\text{for }q<0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|-1}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|\;.

Taking
\delta=0
,
\gamma\geq-\frac{1}{2}
, and for
n\geq 4
, we obtain

\displaystyle|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|\Big)\cdot\begin{cases}C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{2+\gamma}},\quad\text{for }q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}},\,\quad\text{for }q<0,\end{cases}

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi|\Big)\cdot\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{for }q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}},\,\quad\text{for }q<0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|\Big)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|\Big)\cdot c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|-1}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|\;.

Hence,

\displaystyle|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|\Big)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|\Big)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|\;.

Consequently,

\displaystyle|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|^{2}\Big)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}(1+|q|)^{3}}

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|^{2}\Big)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}\cdot(1+|q|)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\;.

15.1.
Using the Hardy type inequality to estimate the commutator term

Lemma 15.5
.

Let
w
be defined as in Definition
9.2
, where
\gamma>0
.
Let
\Phi
a tensor that decays fast enough at spatial infinity for all time
t
, such that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}\cdot(1+|q|)}\cdot w\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;.

Then, since
\gamma\neq 0
, we have for
0\leq a\leq n-1
,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|q|)^{a}(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot|\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|q|)^{a}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\;.

Proof.

Based on the Hardy type inequality that we showed in Corollary
14.1
, by taking
R(\Omega)=0
for all
\Omega\in{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}
, we have for
\gamma\neq 0
and for
0\leq a\leq n-1
, that if

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{a}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(15.6)

then,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=0}^{r=\infty}\frac{1}{(1+t+r)^{a}(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}drd\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=0}^{r=\infty}\frac{1}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}drd\sigma^{n-1}\;.

Thus,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+r)^{a}(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma,\mu)\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>\cdot w\;.

We have

\displaystyle 1+t+r\sim 1+t+|q|\;.

(15.9)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|q|)^{a}(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+r)^{a}(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot<\Phi,\Phi>\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+r)^{a}}\cdot<\partial_{r}\Phi,\partial_{r}\Phi>\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|q|)^{a}}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r}\Phi,{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r}\Phi>\cdot w\;.

We will use now the Hardy type inequality to estimate the commutator term.

Lemma 15.6
.

For
n\geq 4
, let
H
such that for all time
t
, for
\gamma\neq 0
and
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}\cdot w(q)\cdot|H|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(15.10)

and let
h
such that for all time
t
, for all
|K|\leq|I|
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+|q|)}\cdot w(q)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(15.11)

then, for
\delta=0
, for either
\Phi=H
or
\Phi=A
, using the bootstrap assumption on
\Phi
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot c(\gamma)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|^{2}\big)\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt
