\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt\;.

Proof.

Based on what we have shown in Lemma
15.5
, for
H
decaying fast enough at spatial infinity, for
\gamma\neq 0
and
0\leq a\leq n-1
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=0}^{r=\infty}\frac{1}{(1+t+|q|)^{a}(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|^{2}\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}drd\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\int_{r=0}^{r=\infty}\frac{1}{(1+t+|q|)^{a}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H)|^{2}\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}drd\sigma^{n-1}\;.

Hence, for
n\geq 4
, for
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we have
2-\lambda<2\leq n-1
and therefore

\displaystyle C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|^{2}\Big)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}(1+|q|)^{3}}\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}c(\gamma)\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H)|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}}\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H)|^{2}\cdot w\;.

Based on what we showed in Lemma
15.4
, we have for
n\geq 4
,
\delta=0
,
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H|^{2}\Big)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}(1+|q|)^{3}}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|^{2}\Big)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H)|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\;.

However, based on what we showed in Lemma
5.1
, and by lowering indices with respect to the metric
m
, we have

\displaystyle H_{\mu\nu}=-h_{\mu\nu}+O_{\mu\nu}(h^{2}).

thus, for all
|I|
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h_{\mu\nu}+\sum_{|J|+|K|\leq|I|}O_{\mu\nu}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)

and hence,

\displaystyle{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H_{\mu\nu})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{a}{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h_{\mu\nu}+\sum_{|J|+|K|\leq|I|}O_{\mu\nu}({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)\cdot{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)\;.

Consequently,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)|+\sum_{|J|+|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\alpha}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|\;.

We obtain,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)|+\sum_{|J|+|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)|+\sum_{|J|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor\;,\;|K|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|+\sum_{|J|\leq|I|\;,\;|K|\leq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|\;.

We have shown in Lemma
10.1
and Lemma
10.4
, that for
n\geq 4
,
\delta=0
, we have for all
|I|
,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\;,

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}\;.

Hence,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)|+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|\cdot C(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|\cdot C(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\Big(1+C(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\epsilon\Big)\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}C(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|\;.

Consequently,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H)|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\Big(1+C(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\epsilon\Big)^{2}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}C(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}\cdot(1+|q|)}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\;.

Based on the Hardy-type inequality that we showed in Lemma
15.5
, we get that if
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h
is such that for all time
t
, for
n\geq 4
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(15.14)

then, for
\gamma\neq 0
,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\cdot w\;.

Hence, for
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h
decaying fast enough, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H)|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(1+C(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\epsilon\Big)^{2}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}C(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(1+C(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\epsilon\Big)^{2}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}c(\gamma)\cdot C(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\epsilon\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|^{2}\cdot w\;.

Finally, we obtain for small
E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\epsilon\leq 1
, and for
\Phi
and
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h
,
|J|\leq|I|
, decaying fast enough at spatial infinity, the following energy estimate

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w

(15.15)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}H)|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle C(|I|)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|^{2}\big)\cdot w\Big)

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\;.

Remark 15.1
.

It is straightforward to see that if we restrict ourselves to the case
n\geq 5
, excluding
n=4
, this would relax slightly the decay assumption on spatial infinity for
H
, and we obtain the following lemma.

Lemma 15.7
.

For
n\geq 5
, let
H
such that for all time
t
, for
\gamma\neq 0
, and
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{3-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot|H|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(15.17)

and let
h
such that for all time
t
, for all
|K|\leq|I|
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(15.18)

then, for
\delta=0
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{3-\lambda}}\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot c(\gamma)

\displaystyle\times\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|^{2}\big)\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt\;.

16.
The energy estimate for
n\geq 4

Corollary 16.1
.

For
n\geq 4
,
\delta=0
, we have for all
|I|
,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H)(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\;,

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}\;.

Proof.

We showed in Lemma
15.2
, that for all
|I|
,
\delta\leq\frac{(n-2)}{2}
,
\epsilon\leq 1
,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H)(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

and

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}-\delta}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}},\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Taking
\delta=0
, we have
0\leq\frac{(4-2)}{2}=1\leq\frac{(n-2)}{2}
, for
n\geq 4
. Assume also that the energy is small such that
\epsilon\leq 1
. We get that for
\gamma\geq-\frac{1}{2}
,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H)(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\;,

and for
\gamma\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}H(t,x)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}},\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c(\delta)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot E(|I|+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}\;.

Lemma 16.1
.

Let
w
as in Definition
9.2
, where
\gamma>0
.
Then, since
\gamma>-\frac{1}{2}
, we have for all
q
,

\displaystyle w^{\prime}(q)\geq 0\;,

and

\displaystyle w^{\prime}(q)\leq\frac{w(q)}{(1+|q|)}\;.

Proof.

We have from Definition
9.2
, that
\gamma>0
and that

\displaystyle w(q)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}(1+q)^{1+2\gamma}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
1\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

We compute,

\displaystyle w^{\prime}(q)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}(1+2\gamma)(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
0\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}(1+2\gamma)\frac{w(q)}{(1+|q|)}\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
0\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Hence, since
\gamma>-\frac{1}{2}
, we have
w^{\prime}(q)\geq 0
. Also, for
q>0
, we have

\displaystyle w^{\prime}(q)\sim\frac{w(q)}{(1+|q|)}\;.

For
q<0
, we have

\displaystyle w^{\prime}(q)\leq\frac{1}{(1+|q|)}=\frac{w(q)}{(1+|q|)}\;.

Therefore, for all
q
,

\displaystyle w^{\prime}(q)\leq\frac{w(q)}{(1+|q|)}\;.

Lemma 16.2
.

Let
n\geq 4
. Assume that
H^{\mu\nu}=g^{\mu\nu}-m^{\mu\nu}
satisfies

\displaystyle|H|\leq C<\frac{1}{n}\;,\quad\text{where $n$ is the space dimension},

(16.1)

and assume that in wave coordinates
\{t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n} \}
, we have for
j
running over spatial indices
\{x^{1},\ldots,x^{n} \}
, for all time
t
,

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{rj}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{j}\Phi>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0

(16.2)

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{tr}\cdot<\partial_{t}\Phi,\partial_{t}\Phi>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;.

(16.3)

Then, for
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
, for
\gamma>-\frac{1}{2}
and
\mu\neq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)}{(1+t)^{1+\lambda}}\cdot\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt\;.

Proof.

Let
\Phi_{\mu\nu}
be a tensor solution of the following tensorial wave equation

\displaystyle g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi_{\mu\nu}=S_{\mu\nu}\,,

(16.5)

where
S_{\mu\nu}
is the source term, with a sufficiently smooth metric
g
, with
\Phi
satisfying the assumptions of the lemma. Then, based on Lemma
13.4
, we have the following

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}-2<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi,S>\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|H|\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot|w^{\prime}(q)|

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r}\Phi,{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r}\Phi>

\displaystyle+\delta^{ij}<({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{i}-\frac{x_{i}}{r}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r})\Phi,({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{j}-\frac{x_{j}}{r}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r})\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\;,

where the integration on
\Sigma_{t}
is taken with respect to the measure
dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}
, and the integration in
t
is taken with respect to the measure
dt
.

However, we showed in Lemma
16.1
, that for
\gamma\neq-\frac{1}{2}
, we have
w^{\prime}(q)\geq 0
, thus we can ignore on the right hand side of the inequality the negative term

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r}\Phi,{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r}\Phi>

\displaystyle+\delta^{ij}<({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{i}-\frac{x_{i}}{r}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r})\Phi,({\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{j}-\frac{x_{j}}{r}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r})\Phi>\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)\;.

Hence, we get for
n\geq 4
,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}2|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi|\cdot|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot|w^{\prime}(q)|

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi+{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r}\Phi|^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{i}-\frac{x_{i}}{r}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{r})\Phi|^{2}\Big)\cdot w^{\prime}(q)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t)^{1+\lambda}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot|w^{\prime}(q)|

\displaystyle\text{(using $a\cdot b\lesssim a^{2}+b^{2}$)}.

We also showed in Lemma
16.1
, that for
\gamma>-\frac{1}{2}
, we have

\displaystyle w^{\prime}(q)\leq\frac{w(q)}{(1+|q|)}\;.

Thus,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t)^{1+\lambda}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdot|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\;.

Choosing
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)}{(1+t)^{1+\lambda}}\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\;.

16.1.
The main energy estimate for
A
and
h
for
n\geq 4

We now state the main energy estimate that we would like to apply for higher dimensions
n\geq 5
with a bootstrap argument for
\delta=0
. However, the estimate is true for all
n\geq 4
.

Lemma 16.3
.

Assume that
H^{\mu\nu}=g^{\mu\nu}-m^{\mu\nu}
satisfies

\displaystyle|H|\leq C<\frac{1}{n}\;,\quad\text{where $n$ is the space dimension},

(16.6)

and assume that in wave coordinates
\{t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n}\}
, we have for
j
running over spatial indices
\{x^{1},\ldots,x^{n} \}
, for any
I
as in Definition
9.1
, for all time
t
,

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{rj}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi),{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{j}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;,

(16.7)

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{tr}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi),{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;.

(16.8)

Let
n\geq 4
,
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
,
\gamma>0
. Assume that
H
is such that for all time
t
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot|H|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(16.9)

and that
h
is such that for all
|J|\leq|I|
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;.

(16.10)

Then, for either
\Phi=H
or
\Phi=A
, using the bootstrap assumption on
\Phi
, with
\delta=0
, and for
E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\leq 1
, the following energy estimate holds

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}

\displaystyle+C(|I|)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{(1+t)^{1+\lambda}}\cdot\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|^{2}\big)\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt\;.

Proof.

We showed in Lemma
16.2
and in Lemma
15.6
, that under these assumptions on the metric and on the spatial asymptotic behaviour of the field, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)}{(1+t)^{1+\lambda}}\cdot\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle+C(|I|)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot c(\gamma)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|^{2}\big)\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt\;.

Finally, we obtain for small
E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\leq 1
, and for
\Phi
and
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h
,
|J|\leq|I|
, decaying fast enough at spatial infinity, the following energy estimate

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}\Phi)|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}

\displaystyle+C(|I|)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{(1+t)^{1+\lambda}}\cdot\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}\Phi)|^{2}\big)\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{(1+t)^{1+\lambda}}{\epsilon}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}\Phi|^{2}\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot dt\;.

Remark 16.1
.

It is straightforward to see that if we restrict the lemma to
n\geq 5
, then the decay assumption on
H
could be relaxed to become that for
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
,
\gamma>0
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{3-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot|H|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;.

(16.14)

17.
The proof of global stability for
n\geq 5

Now, we fix
n\geq 5
and
\delta=0
.

17.1.
Using the Hardy type inequality for the space-time integrals of the source terms for
n\geq 5

Lemma 17.1
.

For
n\geq 5
,
\delta=0
, we have

\displaystyle(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}\,

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}\;.

Proof.

We showed in Lemma
12.5
, that for
n\geq 5
, we have

\displaystyle(1+t)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}\,

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)^{-1}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

Taking
\delta=0
, we obtain

\displaystyle(1+t)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}\,

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}\cdot(1+|q|)^{-1}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}\cdot(1+|q|)}\;.

Lemma 17.2
.

For
n\geq 5
and
\delta=0
,

\displaystyle(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}h)|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-\lambda}}\;.

Proof.

We showed in Lemma
12.6
, that for
n\geq 5
,

\displaystyle(1+t)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}h)|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-2\delta}\cdot(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}(1+|q|)^{2+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-4\delta}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

Taking
\delta=0
, we obtain

\displaystyle(1+t)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}h)|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}\cdot(1+|q|)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7}}\;.

Lemma 17.3
.

For
n\geq 5
,
\delta=0
, and for fields decaying fast enough at spatial infinity, such that for all time
t
, for all
|K|\leq|I|
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

then, for
\gamma\neq 0
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}\cdot w\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}\ }\cdot\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot w\Big)\cdot dt\;.

Proof.

Based on what we have shown in Lemma
17.1
, for
n\geq 5
,
\delta=0
, we have

\displaystyle(1+t)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}\,

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}(1+|q|)^{2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}\cdot(1+|q|)^{-1}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}\cdot(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}\cdot(1+|q|)}\;.

Hence,

\displaystyle(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}\,

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}\;.

Under the assumption again that
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A
and
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h
decay fast enough at spatial infinity for all time
t
, for all for
|K|\leq|I|
, such that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

we get by then, that for
\gamma\neq 0
and
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
(and therefore
2-\lambda\leq n-1
for
n\geq 5
), that

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot w\;.

As a result,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}\cdot w\Big)\cdot dt\,

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot w\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot w\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot w\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{c(\gamma,\mu)\cdot\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot w\Big)\cdot dt\;.

Lemma 17.4
.

For
n\geq 5
,
\delta=0
, and for fields decaying fast enough at spatial infinity, such that for all time
t
, for
|K|\leq|I|
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

then, for
\gamma\neq 0
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|^{2}\cdot w\Big)\cdot dt

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}\ }\cdot\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot w\Big)\cdot dt\;.

Proof.

We showed in Lemma
17.2
, that for
n\geq 5
,
\delta=0
,

\displaystyle(1+t)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}(1+|q|)^{2+2\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3}\cdot(1+|q|)}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7}(1+|q|)^{2+4\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7}}\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}\Big)\;.

Hence,

\displaystyle(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{3-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{7-\lambda}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{5-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}\;.

Assuming that both
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A
and
{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h
decay fast enough at spatial infinity for all time
t
, i.e. that

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;.

Then, for
\gamma\neq 0
and
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we have
0\leq 2-\lambda\leq 4\leq n-1
, and consequently,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot w

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\frac{1}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}}\cdot\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot w\;.

As a result,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|^{2}\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)^{2}}\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}\ }\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot w

\displaystyle+\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t+|q|)^{4-\lambda}}\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\frac{\epsilon}{(1+t)^{2-\lambda}\ }\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot w\;.

17.2.
Grönwall type inequality on the energy for
n\geq 5

Lemma 17.5
.

Assume that
H^{\mu\nu}=g^{\mu\nu}-m^{\mu\nu}
satisfies

\displaystyle|H|\leq C<\frac{1}{n}\;,\quad\text{where $n$ is the space dimension},

(17.5)

and assume that in wave coordinates
\{t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n} \}
, we have for
j
running over spatial indices
\{x^{1},\ldots,x^{n} \}
, for all
|J|\leq|I|
, for all time
t
,

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{rj}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A),{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{j}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A)>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;,

(17.6)

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{tr}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A),{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A)>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;,

(17.7)

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{rj}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h),{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{j}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;,

(17.8)

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{tr}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h),{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h)>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;.

(17.9)

Let
n\geq 5
,
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
. Assume that
H
is such that for all time
t
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{3-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot|H|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(17.10)

and assume
h
and
A
are such that for
|J|\leq|I|
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(17.11)

and

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;.

Then, for
\gamma\neq 0
and for

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1}(\tau)\leq E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\epsilon\,,

(17.13)

for all
0\leq\tau\leq t
, and for small
E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)
, we have the following energy estimate

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(0)+c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}\ }\cdot{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(\tau)\cdot d\tau\,,

where

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{|I|}(\tau):=\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\big(\|w^{1/2}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h^{1}(t,\cdot))\|_{L^{2}}+\|w^{1/2}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A(t,\cdot))\|_{L^{2}}\big)\,.

Proof.

Based on what we have shown in Lemma
16.3
, under the assumption on the metric, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t}}\big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)|^{2}\big)\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{t=0}}\big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)|^{2}\big)\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}

\displaystyle+C(|I|)\cdot c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}}\cdot\Big(\sum_{|J|\leq|I|}\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}\big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\big)\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot d\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}A|^{2}

\displaystyle+| {\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}g^{{\alpha}{\beta}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\alpha}}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{{\beta}}h|^{2}\Big)\cdot w\cdot dx^{1}\ldots dx^{n}\Big)\cdot d\tau\;.

For
n\geq 5
,
\delta=0
, and for fields decaying fast enough at spatial infinity, such that for all time
t
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+t+r)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}(t)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

then, by Lemmas
17.3
and
17.4
, for
\gamma\neq 0
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|^{2}\big)\cdot w\Big)\cdot d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+\tau)^{2-\lambda}\ }\cdot\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}\sum_{|K|\leq|I|}\Big(|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}A)|^{2}+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{K}}h)|^{2}\Big)\cdot w\Big)\cdot d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+\tau)^{2-\lambda}\ }\cdot{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(\tau)\cdot d\tau\;.

For
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we have
2-\lambda\geq 1+\lambda
and therefore

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{\tau}}(1+t)^{1+\lambda}\cdot\big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}A)|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}(g^{\lambda\mu}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\lambda}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{\mu}h)|^{2}\big)\cdot w\Big)\cdot d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}\ }\cdot{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(\tau)\cdot d\tau\;.

Finally, injecting in (
17.2
), we obtain,

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(0)+c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}\ }\cdot{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(\tau)\cdot d\tau\;.

17.3.
The proof of the theorem for
n\geq 5

Proposition 17.1
.

Let
n\geq 5
and let
N\geq 2\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2
. Assume that for all
I
, as in Definition
9.1
, with
|I|\leq N
, we have in wave coordinates
\{t,x^{1},\ldots,x^{n} \}
, for
j
running over spatial indices
\{x^{1},\ldots,x^{n} \}
, for time
t=0
,

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{rj}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A),{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{j}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;,

(17.17)

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{tr}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A),{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;,

(17.18)

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{rj}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h),{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{j}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;,

(17.19)

\displaystyle\lim_{r\to\infty}\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n}}g^{tr}\cdot<{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h),{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}_{t}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)>\cdot w\cdot r^{n-1}d\sigma^{n-1}=0\;.

(17.20)

Also, assume that for
\gamma>0
and for
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we have for time
t=0
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+r)^{3-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot|H|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(17.21)

and for for all
|I|\leq N
,

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;,

(17.22)

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{S}}^{n-1}}\lim_{r\to\infty}\Big(\frac{r^{n-1}}{(1+r)^{2-\lambda}\cdot(1+|q|)}w(q)\cdot\Big(|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A|^{2}+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h|^{2}\Big)d\sigma^{n-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0\;.

Under these stated assumptions, for any constant
E(N)
(that is there to bound
{\cal E}_{N}(t)
in (
17.26
)), there exist two constants, a constant
c_{1}
that depends on
\gamma>0
and on
n\geq 5
, and a constant
c_{2}
(to bound
\overline{{\cal E}_{N}}(0)
defined in (
1
)), that depends on
E(N)
, on
N\geq 2\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2
and on
w
(i.e. depends on
\gamma
), such that if

\displaystyle\overline{{\cal E}}_{(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)}(0)\leq c_{1}(\gamma,n)\;,

(17.24)

and if

\displaystyle\overline{{\cal E}_{N}}(0)\leq c_{2}(E(N),N,\gamma)\;,

(17.25)

then, we have for all time
t
,

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{N}(t)\leq E(N)\;,

(17.26)

where

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{N}(\tau):=\sum_{|J|\leq N}\big(\|w^{1/2}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}h^{1}(t,\cdot))\|_{L^{2}}+\|w^{1/2}{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{J}}A(t,\cdot))\|_{L^{2}}\big)\;.

Consequently, the initial value Cauchy problem for the Einstein Yang-Mills equations in the Lorenz gauge and in wave coordinates, that we defined in the set-up, will admit a global solution in time
t
for initial data satisfying (
17.24
) and (
17.25
). As a result, in the Lorenz gauge, the Yang-Mills potential decays to zero and the metric decays to the Minkowski metric in wave coordinates. More precisely, for all
|I|\leq N-\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor-1
, we have,

\displaystyle|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A)(t,x)|+|{\nabla^{(\bf{m})}}({\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h)(t,x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\frac{E(N)}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}}(1+|q|)^{1+\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{E(N)}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0,\end{cases}

and

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}A(t,x)|+|{\mathcal{L}}_{Z^{I}}h(t,x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\frac{E(N)}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{E(N)\cdot(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1+t+|q|)^{\frac{(n-1)}{2}}}\,,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

Proof.

We start with the bootstrap assumption explained in Section
9.6
. We have by then, thanks to Lemma
15.1
, for
n\geq 5
and for
\delta=0
, that

\displaystyle|H(t,x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c(\gamma)\cdot\frac{{\cal E}_{(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)}}{(1+t+|q|)^{2}(1+|q|)^{\gamma}},\quad\text{when }\quad q>0,\\
\frac{{\cal E}_{(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)}}{(1+t+|q|)^{2}}(1+|q|)^{\frac{1}{2}},\,\quad\text{when }\quad q<0.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot{\cal E}_{(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot\epsilon

\displaystyle\text{(where we used that we chose $\delta=0$\;, see \eqref{delataqualtozero})}.

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)

(where we used that we chose
\epsilon\leq 1
, see (
9.45
),

and in fact, in this paper, we chose
\epsilon=1
, see (
9.43
)).

By choosing
E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)
small enough, depending on
\gamma
and on
n
(which imposes the condition on the initial data by (
9.39
)), we have

\displaystyle c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)<\frac{1}{n}\;.

(17.27)

In addition, we claim that the decay assumptions on the initial data, stated in the proposition, will propagate in time, under the bootstrap assumption, since the fields satisfy a wave equation, and thus, they will be satisfied for all time
t
. Consequently, we could use Lemma
17.5
, where we fix
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{2}
arbitrary, and we get that

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\cdot{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(0)+c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\epsilon}{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}\ }\cdot{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(\tau)\cdot d\tau\;.

Now, using the celebrated Grönwall lemma, we get

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(t)\leq C\cdot{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(0)\cdot\exp\Big(\int_{0}^{t}c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot\epsilon\cdot\frac{1}{(1+\tau)^{1+\lambda}\ }\cdot d\tau\Big)

(17.28)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\cdot{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(0)\cdot\exp\Big(c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot\epsilon\cdot\Big[\frac{-1}{\lambda(1+\tau)^{\lambda}}\Big]^{\infty}_{0}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\cdot{\cal E}_{|I|}^{2}(0)\cdot\exp\Big(c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot\epsilon\cdot\frac{1}{\lambda}\Big)\;,

which also leads to, using that we chose
\epsilon\leq 1
and that
E(k)\leq 1
(see (
9.45
) and (
9.46
)), that

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{|I|}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\cdot{\cal E}_{|I|}(0)\cdot\exp\Big(c(\gamma)\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot\epsilon\cdot\frac{1}{\lambda}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\cdot{\cal E}_{|I|}(0)\cdot\exp\Big(c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot\frac{1}{\lambda}\Big)\;.

Thus, choosing an initial data such that the energy norm defined in (
1
) satsfies

\displaystyle\overline{{\cal E}_{|I|}}(0)\leq\frac{1}{2\cdot C\cdot\exp\Big(c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot\frac{1}{\lambda}\Big)}\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\;,

(17.29)

implies that

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{|I|}(0)\leq\frac{1}{2\cdot C\cdot\exp\Big(c(\gamma)\cdot C(|I|)\cdot\frac{1}{\lambda}\Big)}\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\;.

(17.30)

This leads to

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{|I|}(t)\leq\frac{1}{2}\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\;.

However, for
|I|\geq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1
, which means for
\frac{|I|}{2}\geq\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1
, we have

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1}(t)\leq{\cal E}_{|I|}(0)\;.

Thus,

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1}(t)\leq\frac{1}{2}\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\;.

(17.31)

This shows that the estimate
{\cal E}_{\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1}(t)\leq E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)
is in fact a true estimate and therefore, we can close the bootstrap argument explained in Section
9.5
, for
{\cal E}_{\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1}(t)
, with
\epsilon=1
and
\delta=0
. For this, we have used the condition that

\displaystyle|I|\geq\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1\;,

which imposes that
|I|\geq 2\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+2
, and we also got that

\displaystyle{\cal E}_{|I|}(t)\leq\frac{1}{2}\cdot E(\lfloor\frac{|I|}{2}\rfloor+\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor+1)\;.

(17.32)

This in turn gives, using Lemmas
10.4
and
10.1
, the stated decay estimates on the fields.
